Description: Develop the Business Partner Management menu under Master Data > Business Partner menu in COMPASS (backoffice) as the single unified page for: (1) submitting New Outlet Order (NOO) requests to register new customer outlets (2) submitting Perubahan Data Customer (PDC) requests, and (3) the approval queue where authorized approver roles review, approve, or reject pending NOO and PDC requests from both COMPASS and SFM Mobile. The approval flow is sequential: CDO → KDC → BAC → MDM.

  - Jabatan*
  - Fill Kontak Owner*, then to check in system

  - Owner ID (read-only)

[AC]   - <w:tcW w:w="4518" w:type="dxa" /><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="BFBFBF" w:themeColor="background1" w:themeShade="BF" w:sz="6" /><w:left w:val="single" w:color="BFBFBF" w:themeColor="background1" w:themeShade="BF" w:sz="6" /><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="BFBFBF" w:themeColor="background1" w:themeShade="BF" w:sz="6" /><w:right w:val="single" w:color="BFBFBF" w:themeColor="background1" w:themeShade="BF" w:sz="6" /></w:tcBorders><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="75" w:type="dxa" /><w:left w:w="135" w:type="dxa" /><w:bottom w:w="75" w:type="dxa" /><w:right w:w="135" w:type="dxa" /></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top" /></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="624607FC" w:rsidP="624607FC" w:rsidRDefault="624607FC" w14:paraId="59DBF5E9" w14:textId="13D9416C"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="ListParagraph" /><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0" /><w:numId w:val="25" /></w:numPr><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Times New Roman" w:hAnsi="Times New Roman" w:eastAsia="Times New Roman" w:cs="Times New Roman" /><w:b w:val="0" /><w:bCs w:val="0" /><w:i w:val="0" /><w:iCs w:val="0" /><w:color w:val="2F2F2F" /><w:sz w:val="24" /><w:szCs w:val="24" /></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="624607FC" w:rsidR="624607FC"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Times New Roman" w:hAnsi="Times New Roman" w:eastAsia="Times New Roman" w:cs="Times New Roman" /><w:b w:val="0" /><w:bCs w:val="0" /><w:i w:val="0" /><w:iCs w:val="0" /><w:strike w:val="0" /><w:dstrike w:val="0" /><w:color w:val="2F2F2F" /><w:sz w:val="24" /><w:szCs w:val="24" /><w:u w:val="none" /><w:lang w:val="en-US" /></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">After completing all fields in Customer Hierarchy, user can continue to Financial & Tax tab.
  - Fields with * sign is mandatory to fill in.
  - Financial & Tax tab must display the following fields in order:
  - Payer  
  - Bill to 
  - Pricelist*
  - Price Group ID*
  - Payment term*
  - PPBJ (PBBJ/Non-PPBJ)
  - Tipe Organisasi (Bendaharwan/BUMN).
  - Nama Organisasi (dropdown)
  - PKP (checkbox).  If PKP checked: Upload SPPKP* (file upload).
  - Toko Meminta Faktur Pajak (checkbox)
  - Tax Classification (Fiscal Position): dropdown.
  - Incoterm
  - Tax Identity (Identitas Pajak). 
  - Tax sama dengan Customer Address (checkbox)
  - Tax ID
  - NPWP Type Option:
  - Perorangan: Upload NPWP and KTP for system checking based on NPWP or KTP (NIK) number.
  - If registered in system, choose NPWP/KTP registered, then address is auto-filled from database record.
  - If not yet registered, show checlist box: “Sudah dipadankan?”. 
  - IF yes: No NPWP filled by NIK.
  - IF no: No NPWP filed by NPWP scan result, and No NIK filled by KTP (NIK). Address field is filled by Address from KTP/NPWP scan result.
  - Badan: Upload foto NPWP Badan (OCR) to check in sytem database.
  - If the NPWP/KTP is registered, the system must "Show data NPWP/KTP sudah terdaftar" and allow "Auto-fill alamat terdaftar" from the database record. 
  - If the NPWP is not registered, the system must perform "Auto-fill Nomor NPWP", “Auto-fill nama NPWP”, “Auto-fill Alamat NPWP” based on extracted OCR data.
  - Group Name (BP Holding)
  - If Tax Identity using existing/registered data: display a recommendation of the group registered under that customer to choose.
  - If NOT: Admin can click “Create New Customer Group” checkbox field
  - Create New Group Name (Checkbox)
  - When "Create New Group" option is selected, the system automatically populate the field with a default value of [Store Name + Group], while still allowing the admin, CDO, and MDM to edit the value manually.
  - No uniqueness validation shall be performed when the auto-populated value is generated (e.g., when the form is first opened). Validation shall occur only when the manually edit or NOO form approve button is clicked.
  - If the New Group Name isn’t unique, it can’t be approved and shall choose the existing one from Group Name field.
  - Checkbox: Memiliki Toko Cabang
  - IF YES: Fill in NITKU.
  - Bounded NITKU
  - If user previously choose “PPBJ”: 
  - Display Bounded NITKU and Bounded Area Street as mandatory fields to be filled in. 
  - Street 2–4: Street 2–4 rows must only appear when the previous name field exceeds the character limit.
  - Rekening:
  - Bank Country
  - Account Number
  - Bank
  - Account Name
  - Account Type

  - Mandatory fields that are empty must display inline error messages on attempted submission.
  - The system must provide a 'Save Draft' action that saves current data without mandatory field validation.
  - The system must provide a 'Approve' action that triggers a confirmation dialog before final submission.
  - Upon confirmation, the NOO is submitted with status 'Waiting Approval CDO'.
  - The relevant CDO approver must receive the new pending NOO.
  - After successful submission, the user is returned to the BP Management page with a success notification.
  - After submission, the NOO form becomes read-only until rejected.

  - The log must include:
  - Timestamp
  - Action (Submitted / Approved / Auto Approved / Rejected)
  - Actor name and role
  - Rejection reason (if applicable)
  - The log must be sorted from newest to oldest.

  - If the request is rejected, the system must display the rejection reason.
  - The rejection reason must appear in the current History & Approval Log.

[AC]   - <w:tcW w:w="4518" w:type="dxa" /><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="BFBFBF" w:themeColor="background1" w:themeShade="BF" w:sz="6" /><w:left w:val="single" w:color="BFBFBF" w:themeColor="background1" w:themeShade="BF" w:sz="6" /><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="BFBFBF" w:themeColor="background1" w:themeShade="BF" w:sz="6" /><w:right w:val="single" w:color="BFBFBF" w:themeColor="background1" w:themeShade="BF" w:sz="6" /></w:tcBorders><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="75" w:type="dxa" /><w:left w:w="135" w:type="dxa" /><w:bottom w:w="75" w:type="dxa" /><w:right w:w="135" w:type="dxa" /></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top" /></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="624607FC" w:rsidP="624607FC" w:rsidRDefault="624607FC" w14:paraId="33560772" w14:textId="68322F46"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="ListParagraph" /><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0" /><w:numId w:val="50" /></w:numPr><w:spacing w:before="200" w:beforeAutospacing="off" w:after="200" w:afterAutospacing="off" /><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Times New Roman" w:hAnsi="Times New Roman" w:eastAsia="Times New Roman" w:cs="Times New Roman" /><w:b w:val="0" /><w:bCs w:val="0" /><w:i w:val="0" /><w:iCs w:val="0" /><w:sz w:val="24" /><w:szCs w:val="24" /></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="624607FC" w:rsidR="624607FC"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Times New Roman" w:hAnsi="Times New Roman" w:eastAsia="Times New Roman" w:cs="Times New Roman" /><w:b w:val="0" /><w:bCs w:val="0" /><w:i w:val="0" /><w:iCs w:val="0" /><w:strike w:val="0" /><w:dstrike w:val="0" /><w:sz w:val="24" /><w:szCs w:val="24" /><w:u w:val="none" /><w:lang w:val="en-US" /></w:rPr><w:t>The NOO detail request page form must provide an 'Approve' button.
  - Upon approval by CDO: status changes to 'Waiting Approval KDC'; KDC approver is notified.
  - Upon approval by KDC: status changes to 'Waiting Approval BAC'; BAC approver is notified.
  - BAC Approval two cases:
  - Upon approval by BAC (manual): status changes to 'Waiting Approval MDM'; MDM approver is notified.
  - Upon BAC auto-approval (Pricing & Discount match group): status changes to 'Waiting Approval MDM' automatically; actor logged as 'System – Auto Approval'.
  - Upon approval by MDM (final): NOO data is committed to SAP/ERP; status changes to 'Approved / Active'; submitter is notified.
  - Each approval action must be logged in the History & Approval Log: timestamp, approver role, approver name, action (Approved), comment (if any).
  - Approved request must no longer appear as actionable in the Approval List for that role.

  - The BP Type selection step must show four options: Customer, Tax, Owner, Holding. 
  - After user selects a BP Type, the system must display a BP search field filtered to that BP Type only.
  - The search must support at least: Customer ID / Tax ID / Owner ID, SAP/ERP ID, and BP Name.
  - Search results must only show BP records matching the selected BP Type.
  - User must select one BP from the search results before proceeding.
  - After selecting a BP, the system must open the PDC form pre-populated with the current data of the selected BP.
  - The PDC form structure must match the selected BP Type.
  - Users without PDC permission must not see the PDC option in the Request popup.

If no fields are changed, the system must block submission and show an appropriate message.
  - Mandatory fields that are cleared must display inline error messages on attempted submission.
  - The system must provide a 'Submit' action that triggers a confirmation dialog showing a summary of changed fields before final submission.
  - Upon confirmation, the PDC request is submitted with status 'Waiting Approval CDO'.
  - The relevant CDO approver must receive an in-app notification of the new pending PDC, including the BP Type being updated.
  - After successful submission, the user is returned to the BP Management page with a success notification.

  - When a NOO/PDC is finally approved, the original submitter must be notified.
  - When a NOO/PDC is rejected, the original submitter must be notified with the rejection reason.
  - Notification content must include: Request ID, Tipe Request, Nama Toko, Action Taken, Current Stage.

  - Approving or rejecting without completing mandatory fields must show inline validation errors.
  - The system must prevent partial or broken form rendering on backend failure.
